## Metadata
- Author: [[Jaskaran from The Social Juice]]
- Full Title: This Week in Marketing: Pinterest Predicts, Reels Algorithm, AI-Powered Dating, and Disney Slop
- Category: #articles
- Summary: This week’s marketing news is all about AI reshaping ads, content, and platforms—from new models and creator-rights fights to AI-driven features across Google, Meta, and TikTok. Big deals and disputes stand out, like Disney’s OpenAI tie-up, publisher pushback over AI content, and platform updates for shopping, Reels, and CTV. Marketers face growing complexity as personalization, privacy, and platform power collide.

##### Does your [social presence](https://substack.com/redirect/b84609f5-ca67-4247-ae4e-a3e8b90395e9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) shape AI’s answers?

Discover how your brand’s social presence impacts AI-generated answers, and which platforms matter most. [This research](https://substack.com/redirect/b84609f5-ca67-4247-ae4e-a3e8b90395e9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is grounded in data from platform documentation, third-party studies, and large-scale usage data. No hype. No speculation.

* A former tech executive killed his mother. Her family says [ChatGPT](https://substack.com/redirect/e29992b0-55c0-4528-8456-7b896cac32a0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) made her a target.
* [7 Australian teens](https://substack.com/redirect/a64eaa55-9f32-4096-b22f-05798255b452?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) react to the country’s social media ban.

* TikTok narrows the [measurement gap](https://substack.com/redirect/ae49dddc-2eec-4014-aba1-70db58a168e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with DoubleVerify partnership.
* [TikTok](https://substack.com/redirect/686a5ff2-432d-4a35-a18f-859a2bf2cb6d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reveals the Top Artists and Songs of 2025.
	+ KATSEYE named [TikTok’s Global Artist of the Year](https://substack.com/redirect/0e5d1ea2-e238-496e-a8ce-fb911660749a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for 2025.
* TikTok announces “[TikTok In The Mix](https://substack.com/redirect/74f5c2d2-43cc-47d9-b0be-428b4d84f0a0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)”, a major new LIVE podcast series, sponsored by T-Mobile.
* TikTok Shop partners with [ReBound](https://substack.com/redirect/f10849af-be11-48f5-be73-4d3be77b2f50?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for social commerce returns.
* You can use [Instacart on ChatGPT](https://substack.com/redirect/f01c73bb-0063-498f-9b10-9a2bef7b760b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to make a viral TikTok recipe, but I wouldn’t recommend it.
* TikTok [publishes](https://substack.com/redirect/f08a6a20-36a4-4521-b71c-382a6d3eb0fa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) new Holiday gift guide.
* Explaining TikTok’s ‘[Turn the Lights Off](https://substack.com/redirect/7069401b-c30b-4493-9bfd-d0d6d5599376?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ trend, and what it’s got to do with vibey Jon Hamm.
* Videos of sexually suggestive, [AI-generated children](https://substack.com/redirect/dc8dc780-826d-45de-97b9-ec30c5f2d060?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are racking up millions of likes on TikTok, study finds.
* All TikTok wants for [Christmas](https://substack.com/redirect/28e54b6f-5eb5-479c-921b-727e8cf5adaa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is a Mariah Carey concert.

> TikTok officially enters the [micro-dramas business](https://substack.com/redirect/cbb4d9fb-3424-46a2-bc22-f1bf527c161d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with launch of “Minis,” in-app mini programs.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!dE8f!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fc0b0e67a-ad8c-4778-a9ee-8066b4dda5be_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/4c0b332e-ac12-4038-9a33-0484a1ee9a9a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Instagram now allows you to [reshare public stories](https://substack.com/redirect/1d624206-01e4-4ae4-b9a1-64b2fff11153?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to your own story.
* Instagram is generating [inaccurate SEO bait](https://substack.com/redirect/b80fab97-4ae4-4f2c-842d-064da6b02964?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for your posts.
* ChatGPT and [Threads](https://substack.com/redirect/4e8e599a-9340-492f-9204-364f38f73412?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are Apple’s most downloaded apps of 2025 in the US.
* Instagram launches [Carversations](https://substack.com/redirect/546676dd-a033-445e-8909-3d8bf11d0090?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), a new video series that spotlights authentic conversations between parents and teens.
* [Edits App](https://substack.com/redirect/5e51da0a-f241-487d-8b2b-c026e6aa60a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) adds storyboards, templates for trending videos, and “add reels” feature.
* Threads tests a Instagram-like visual interface for the [post uploading](https://substack.com/redirect/1d6e3543-e0af-421c-b5a7-ecd8bf7207d7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) process.

> 🦀 Report Break: [Global advertising spend](https://substack.com/redirect/f65b3e76-25ba-4993-ad78-f8dc77bd08dc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is now on course to close out 2025 with growth of almost 9% to $1.19 trillion, an upgrade of 1.5 percentage points (pp) from WARC’s September forecast.

> Next year, total global advertising is forecast to reach $1.3 trillion at a year on year growth rate of 9.1%. Growth fo 7.9% in 2027 would push the advertising market’s total value to 1.4 trillion: a doubling in size since the pandemic and equivalent to $150 spent for every person alive today.

* Meta pledge to use [less personal data for ads](https://substack.com/redirect/66dbc92e-e1e4-47da-b717-3bda8f96b570?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) gets EU nod, avoids daily fines.

* [Meta](https://substack.com/redirect/41594314-dd14-4abf-859e-4a4d5d1a6073?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) streamlines [brands’ creator partnerships](https://substack.com/redirect/1f230646-fa34-4bd9-878e-957e401d6671?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) with AI-powered updates.
* WhatsApp is trying to [reinvent](https://substack.com/redirect/94aa2bfc-ac01-48a5-a5a7-a3932f3ac180?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) voicemail.
* Meta’s new [A.I. superstars](https://substack.com/redirect/bc2c1082-5349-42e8-96bc-0041b70bd491?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) are chafing against the rest of the company.
* Meta scopes out site for data center campus in [Finland](https://substack.com/redirect/003ff75b-deb4-4558-bb2e-270eaf929cf5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* [WhatsApp](https://substack.com/redirect/7cc0c8b5-3e04-47f4-be29-098647bca713?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) announces new updates: Missed call messages, fun Status stickers, improved Meta AI image generation, and more.
	+ WhatsApp is testing a feature that lets users apply [strict security settings](https://substack.com/redirect/c4b54260-7d8e-45c7-89db-9c49b7af43b6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to their accounts.
* Meta shuts down [global accounts](https://substack.com/redirect/963dca31-e432-454b-8f2d-121aabce92b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) linked to abortion advice and queer content.

* Ray-Ban Meta glasses take off but face [privacy](https://substack.com/redirect/3679ebbe-39bc-4cdb-9bd6-9a38d5be1be4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and competition test.

* Meta shares [new insights](https://substack.com/redirect/c26c6bf6-9085-4800-ad1a-09455e2a0693?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on Asia Pacific Social Trends 2026.

> ☕️ Report Break: 4.4m people living in ‘[news deserts](https://substack.com/redirect/9ea15b52-7e9b-4955-bf9f-b2fc718f8996?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ says report. 27 council areas are ‘absolute’ news deserts, with no local news provider at all, while a further ten are ‘relative’ deserts, covered by multi-district news outlets.

> The report found that 22 local news outlets had closed over the past year, with a similar number of new ones being launched.

* Agentic coding comes to [Slack](https://substack.com/redirect/73e7ea00-40be-4407-8a83-d69bc70a8d9b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as Anthropic launches Claude Code integration.

* Opera wants you to [pay $20 a month](https://substack.com/redirect/9970f534-c1d1-4ac8-9480-8595e0f5a4ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to use its AI-powered browser Neon.
* Cursor [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/1a4704d5-bdb6-4370-a698-33d644814b0f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) visual editor for web app development in browser.
* Indian AI royalty proposal targets [data practices](https://substack.com/redirect/36e4ad54-3a7d-48ee-b629-c39eac9f6a9c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) of OpenAI, Google.
* Kroger [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/e3e9161d-dc09-4404-897d-bd395cc2e357?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) AI-generated email digest for suppliers.
* How publisher cohorts help [sites band together](https://substack.com/redirect/d38c0199-f64f-409a-a8ee-7504df6c4ffd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to fight traffic-stealing AI.
* Z AI [releases](https://substack.com/redirect/543c3936-1edd-4476-a959-9db10f6ef14b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) GLM-4.6V and GLM-4.6V-Flash open models.
* [Outset](https://substack.com/redirect/67040853-61dc-4920-8899-336d72bb5117?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), all-in-one AI-powered research platform secures $30M to expand AI customer experience platform.

---

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!0cUN!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F79af7349-0432-4bf9-83ad-f76d0fd2a362_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/6d6fc1bc-6a50-4fcb-b7d5-a9691b242b27?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* Microsoft Copilot is getting [personal](https://substack.com/redirect/be8901a6-55a1-4786-ab13-7ab3dd42e1d4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ [How people use](https://substack.com/redirect/c8f6d762-bada-445b-abe6-2de770572b29?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Microsoft Copilot depends on whether they’re at a desk or on their phone.
* [LinkedIn](https://substack.com/redirect/ba4f9124-c291-42be-8f1f-dde321cf9359?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) introduces [Reserved Ads](https://substack.com/redirect/5ae78ad4-5661-4898-9eb9-d95a3e1d5df3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), ad personalization, new AI tools.
* [Microsoft 365](https://substack.com/redirect/4d08b34a-b245-4e36-867e-b924243853c0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to include more AI tools – at a higher price.
* OK, what’s going on with [LinkedIn’s algo](https://substack.com/redirect/43607471-0089-4e14-a816-3477ecf79650?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)?
* £2.1bn lawsuit against Microsoft over [cloud licensing](https://substack.com/redirect/31f2ff1a-8a30-46f8-a345-7f369c06cc13?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) heads to court in UK.
* Microsoft announces [$7.5 billion investment](https://substack.com/redirect/887622f0-efe4-4801-8226-f15a96ad0ae7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to strengthen Canadian AI.
	+ Microsoft to invest [$17.5 billion](https://substack.com/redirect/a28c7add-7cee-4b41-a702-09cd4d3d8f3c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in India, CEO Nadella says.
* Bing tests Google-style “[Sponsored results”](https://substack.com/redirect/8d7c2273-11bd-4362-872d-62d6da0c09f0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) grouping.
* LinkedIn is making it easier to integrate [its verification badge](https://substack.com/redirect/f9be5dc2-3443-4076-826e-2a6c7e45a1a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on outside platforms.
	+ LinkedIn [reaches](https://substack.com/redirect/56841fb9-0cf6-4d9b-b46c-83b04dd4c2b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) 100M verified profiles.
* Microsoft unveils [7 AI trends](https://substack.com/redirect/c499596b-eac4-44ef-8d65-7675746b3944?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for 2026.
* Death in the dollhouse as [Microsoft marketing](https://substack.com/redirect/04640f9d-1881-4634-a227-0ef0a0c03771?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) reboots digital soap operas.
* Microsoft AI CEO Mustafa Suleyman says he [won’t match](https://substack.com/redirect/ee1a2af2-912f-4e08-b5c1-a021ade4d3d5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Meta’s high-flying pay packages.

> 🥀 Report Break: Roughly [1 in 5 U.S. teens say they are on TikTok and YouTube](https://substack.com/redirect/271fbe08-be7f-4c7d-8a27-43c06becfc93?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) almost constantly. At the same time, 64% of teens say they use chatbots, including about 3 in 10 who do so daily.

> [![A line chart showing that A majority of teens continue to use YouTube, TikTok, Instagram and Snapchat](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!del9!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Faa25d175-af31-44d4-be2d-8a52123b2cc5_844x674.png "A line chart showing that A majority of teens continue to use YouTube, TikTok, Instagram and Snapchat")](https://substack.com/redirect/85092273-e8f3-4591-9553-62c90caff779?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)

---

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!WMjD!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F605790ce-ce9b-44a1-a77e-bc12503caa70_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/87d9e81e-37ec-4f9e-9551-4f879a3888c7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* That personalized email about loving and [marketing your book](https://substack.com/redirect/3f632492-8744-450c-a63f-3977bb0a4dc6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is a scam.
* Serviceplan Group launches [House of Communication UK](https://substack.com/redirect/fa3f27a7-c103-4e1b-8cd8-3a615ffc7fce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), uniting its media, creative, and technology capabilities under one roof.
* [Channel 4](https://substack.com/redirect/4a6727b4-1a0c-434c-8078-ad9e8cb88415?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) appoints Sky’s Ads Chief Priya Dogra as CEO.
* [Irving Azoff-backed PR companies](https://substack.com/redirect/8022caa4-b795-4e0d-9083-2b21a4f3156b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) rebrand, consolidate under new banner.
* [Berlusconi’s MFE](https://substack.com/redirect/98cac3f2-f0bf-4f67-ac1f-4c21a94fd856?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) backs Paramount over Netflix in Warner Bros ownership battle.
* Barb unveils [cloud-based Data Hub](https://substack.com/redirect/af7b67d5-5a06-4428-a7b6-41334ca31668?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for viewing metrics.
* More Ads? [2026 World Cup](https://substack.com/redirect/a2140a1a-a0a0-4cd2-b027-d0113669cd65?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to feature three-minute hydration breaks in every half throughout tournament.
* Equativ launches [Media Planning Agent](https://substack.com/redirect/4b0ae860-171d-4e72-b16d-a07c39db1b54?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to enhance buyer efficiency.
* IAB Tech Lab introduces [new CTV ad formats](https://substack.com/redirect/6703fda0-56a3-4d73-aefa-1531f85038ac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), updates programmatic guidance.
* [Attain and Yahoo DSP](https://substack.com/redirect/32243272-936a-4b1c-8e23-b656076c42ed?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) deepen partnership to make real-time purchase data available in Yahoo DSP.
* Clearcast [launches](https://substack.com/redirect/f3469d74-c30f-4b7f-a2cf-bd659abf9547?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) API Data Portal.
* Adtech company Teads is [laying off](https://substack.com/redirect/4f9b3325-07a9-4c06-8fa9-6dfcc9e1afda?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) staff 10 months after closing its merger with Outbrain.
* [PubMatic-BrightLine deal](https://substack.com/redirect/9cda5ef7-2a01-489d-8417-85d5143c9525?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) scales interactive CTV ads.
* [Adform](https://substack.com/redirect/3042cf57-aaa0-4da6-9d37-c7ff5158b897?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and RTL AdAlliance join forces for programmatic addressable TV in Europe.
* WPP also upgraded its [ad growth projection for 2025](https://substack.com/redirect/4d2c0ece-69f9-43e3-bb19-33dfb0428298?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), with full year growth now expected to reach 8.8%, a significant improvement on the 7.7% growth which was predicted this time last year.
* So you want to paywall? ([read](https://substack.com/redirect/fa84af09-13f1-423a-9171-125022400e26?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g))
* Home Depot builds bridge to influencers with [creator portal](https://substack.com/redirect/b2e34334-c76a-4dab-af55-cf3c864600db?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Stagwell launches [NewVoices.ai](https://substack.com/redirect/2af8d16c-e1bb-40c7-96b2-d6921c09f3aa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) - An enterprise sales, support and retention platform.

Back to…

***The Social Juice+** includes access to newsletter archive and strategy newsletters. **It’s your decision, But you are losing all three benefits**. When you can also expense it:* [Generate](https://substack.com/redirect/a95714c8-2d9d-4125-b3f3-165e2685c670?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) an Employee Expense Email for Your Manager.

<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money–you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>US TikTok investors in limbo as deal set to be </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6bd87ef5-a837-4a18-8f6d-71c98a25d2ec?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">delayed again</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok narrows the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ae49dddc-2eec-4014-aba1-70db58a168e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">measurement gap</a><span> with DoubleVerify partnership.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/686a5ff2-432d-4a35-a18f-859a2bf2cb6d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok</a><span> reveals the Top Artists and Songs of 2025.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>KATSEYE named </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0e5d1ea2-e238-496e-a8ce-fb911660749a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok’s Global Artist of the Year</a><span> for 2025.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok announces “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/74f5c2d2-43cc-47d9-b0be-428b4d84f0a0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok In The Mix</a><span>”, a major new LIVE podcast series, sponsored by T-Mobile.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok Shop partners with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f10849af-be11-48f5-be73-4d3be77b2f50?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ReBound</a><span> for social commerce returns.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>You can use </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f01c73bb-0063-498f-9b10-9a2bef7b760b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Instacart on ChatGPT</a><span> to make a viral TikTok recipe, but I wouldn’t recommend it.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f08a6a20-36a4-4521-b71c-382a6d3eb0fa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">publishes</a><span> new Holiday gift guide.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Explaining TikTok’s ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7069401b-c30b-4493-9bfd-d0d6d5599376?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Turn the Lights Off</a><span>’ trend, and what it’s got to do with vibey Jon Hamm.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Videos of sexually suggestive, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dc8dc780-826d-45de-97b9-ec30c5f2d060?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI-generated children</a><span> are racking up millions of likes on TikTok, study finds.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>All TikTok wants for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/28e54b6f-5eb5-479c-921b-727e8cf5adaa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Christmas</a><span> is a Mariah Carey concert.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>TikTok officially enters the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cbb4d9fb-3424-46a2-bc22-f1bf527c161d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">micro-dramas business</a><span> with launch of “Minis,” in-app mini programs.</span></p></blockquote>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram now allows you to</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1d624206-01e4-4ae4-b9a1-64b2fff11153?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> reshare public stories</a><span> to your own story. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram is generating </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b80fab97-4ae4-4f2c-842d-064da6b02964?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">inaccurate SEO bait</a><span> for your posts.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>ChatGPT and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4e8e599a-9340-492f-9204-364f38f73412?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Threads</a><span> are Apple’s most downloaded apps of 2025 in the US.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram launches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/546676dd-a033-445e-8909-3d8bf11d0090?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Carversations</a><span>, a new video series that spotlights authentic conversations between parents and teens. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5e51da0a-f241-487d-8b2b-c026e6aa60a5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Edits App</a><span> adds storyboards, templates for trending videos, and “add reels” feature. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Threads tests a Instagram-like visual interface for the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1d6e3543-e0af-421c-b5a7-ecd8bf7207d7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">post uploading</a><span> process.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🦀 Report Break: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f65b3e76-25ba-4993-ad78-f8dc77bd08dc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Global advertising spend</a><span> is now on course to close out 2025 with growth of almost 9% to $1.19 trillion, an upgrade of 1.5 percentage points (pp) from WARC’s September forecast.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Next year, total global advertising is forecast to reach $1.3 trillion at a year on year growth rate of 9.1%. Growth fo 7.9% in 2027 would push the advertising market’s total value to 1.4 trillion: a doubling in size since the pandemic and equivalent to $150 spent for every person alive today.</p>
</blockquote>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta might </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ed01d162-57e2-4a9b-8961-389aa8ce2438?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">charge</a><span> for a future AI model.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta pledge to use</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/66dbc92e-e1e4-47da-b717-3bda8f96b570?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> less personal data for ads</a><span> gets EU nod, avoids daily fines.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta introduces new design improvements to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6c4978ca-30d2-4534-84aa-e32371c186b5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Facebook’s Feed</a><span>, search, and navigation systems.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/41594314-dd14-4abf-859e-4a4d5d1a6073?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meta</a><span> streamlines </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1f230646-fa34-4bd9-878e-957e401d6671?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">brands’ creator partnerships</a><span> with AI-powered updates.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>WhatsApp is trying to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/94aa2bfc-ac01-48a5-a5a7-a3932f3ac180?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reinvent</a><span> voicemail.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta’s new </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bc2c1082-5349-42e8-96bc-0041b70bd491?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">A.I. superstars</a><span> are chafing against the rest of the company.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta scopes out site for data center campus in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/003ff75b-deb4-4558-bb2e-270eaf929cf5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Finland</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google expands </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/61c7d1ea-1fe2-46b4-8aa9-785e9c040c55?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Preferred Sources</a><span> to English users worldwide and launches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8e443dbc-107b-49e3-8db2-40822e9a4ae9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">pilot AI partnerships</a><span> with publishers.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/de4327cd-7f5c-40a5-b21d-075dd9326ac2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">releases</a><span> December 2025 Core update</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google is building </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/03b26d62-78d4-4fbd-ad49-60c5dff8c6b9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">an experimental new browser</a><span> and a new kind of web app.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google launched its </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9ec522da-178a-41b3-a6ba-04d0cb9736c2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">deepest AI research agent</a><span> yet — on the same day OpenAI dropped GPT-5.2.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google tests </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8db0d7c5-f8ac-4e81-8e7b-be9ca701ac32?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">social channel insights</a><span> in Search Console.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google launches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/30004c74-8b2d-4a9f-8858-cf9d5925e798?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">sub-$5 AI Plus plan</a><span> in India to compete with ChatGPT Go.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google updates </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ee5c06a1-88fe-49c3-9f98-f5ec04be800f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Search Live</a><span> with Gemini model upgrade.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/093a1de5-cfa7-4088-976c-fb21cdfcd1c3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">releases</a><span> Gemini Deep Research agent on APIs.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Cadent collaborates with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/35cb3c47-f071-42b4-ab38-78a67026a47d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google Cloud</a><span> to bring data-driven intelligence to advertisers.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google Translate expands </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d4fadb51-671e-4277-b34d-43875febcf55?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">live translation</a><span> to all earbuds on Android.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google launches natural language Developer Assistant for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/087567cf-0768-48b5-917f-0ca4c1a242fb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Google Ads API</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a606002a-1268-4877-9e9b-c6db2c737578?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">confirms</a><span> smaller core updates happen continuously.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google launches</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e2da5ada-81f7-43b1-83fc-5d90b58bd6c7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> managed MCP servers</a><span> that let AI agents simply plug into its tools.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Google AI cites retailers 4% vs. ChatGPT at 36%: </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/74112338-cf68-4d80-84f5-e0d6da86a5db?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Data</a><span>.</span></p></li>

<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>☕️ Report Break: 4.4m people living in ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9ea15b52-7e9b-4955-bf9f-b2fc718f8996?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">news deserts</a><span>’ says report. 27 council areas are ‘absolute’ news deserts, with no local news provider at all, while a further ten are ‘relative’ deserts, covered by multi-district news outlets.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">The report found that 22 local news outlets had closed over the past year, with a similar number of new ones being launched.</p>
</blockquote>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Elon Musk’s X bans </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d21f3bc7-8ff2-49da-8458-7a69b7169984?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">European Commission</a><span> from making ads after €120m fine.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Musk’s X posts </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/8c12a937-1a96-477d-87b4-3bebc4f667af?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">higher sales</a><span> amid costly turnaround.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Operation Bluebird wants to relaunch “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/72f4004d-3e12-4851-8ad6-ea84d9d8e993?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Twitter</a><span>,” says Musk abandoned the name and logo.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X is working on redesigning the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/780daf9a-6808-414b-8bdb-7f7d991bf84c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">notifications page</a><span> on iOS.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Elon Musk teams with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d51c106f-317d-4af7-8d2e-46ecd239f8ce?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">El Salvador</a><span> to bring Grok chatbot to public schools.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span> X for iOS update introduces “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/28ec69d8-eec3-495f-b9ea-5bc18e7b9330?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Creator Studio</a><span>”, a replacement for the “Creator Monetization Dashboard.”</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Trump Admin is preparing to revoke </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e962d8b3-0c79-4a15-8b82-4e1417d81061?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">visas of critics</a><span> of Elon Musk’s Twitter.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Investors say Elon Musk’s SpaceX trading debut will be ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9c7fd204-c237-472a-ae51-010d5a04aeeb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">craziest IPO</a><span>’ ever.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c60ed0ef-c152-4b77-9c4a-3d697db5be90?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Grok</a><span>, now built into Teslas for navigation, says it would run over a billion children to avoid hitting Elon Musk.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X is preparing to launch a new </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ed2730f0-8954-4441-8f1d-674e65d2f0c6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Rare Handles feature</a><span> within its Handles Market.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>X for iOS finally offers </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f09759ec-2659-4715-9ea6-7ffecda6dfd7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">widgets</a><span>, five years after first teasing them.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🫩 Report Break: 6 in 10 unaware of </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/fff39f9e-a03f-4623-b3c0-e9d05ffbe1a2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">UK’s new food ad rules</a><span> as health debate heats up.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Once informed, however, two-thirds (67%) support the measures and nearly three-quarters (74%) believe they will help promote healthier eating habits, suggesting a strong public appetite for change, if people know about it.</p>
</blockquote>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5bc30fda-741a-40ff-a33e-f85fdc1c074a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">We’re not just going to want to be fed AI slop for 16 hours a day</a><span>’: Analyst sees Disney/OpenAI deal as a dividing line in entertainment history.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>IBM to acquire data streaming platform </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0d3a89f3-09c1-49cd-a638-75640ba9a224?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Confluent</a><span> for $11B.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>ChatGPT nears </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b2d48869-7a22-4981-bda0-31c731021943?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">900 Million weekly active users</a><span> but Gemini is catching up.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Internal OpenAI data shows </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0d954339-e6df-44f8-98e9-a75cc6cd9d3b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">ChatGPT</a><span> increasingly surfaces publisher content while eliminating the need for users to click through.</span></p></li></ul>

</li>

<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>🥀 Report Break: Roughly </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/271fbe08-be7f-4c7d-8a27-43c06becfc93?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">1 in 5 U.S. teens say they are on TikTok and YouTube</a><span> almost constantly. <ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
